De vita libri tres (Three Books on Life)
Marsilio Ficino (1433-1499) (1489)
Edition staged: Venice, 1498 edition (5th ed.; first published Florence: Antonio Miscomini, 3 Dec 1489). Biblioteca Nazionale Centrale di Firenze scan via Internet Archive (ita-bnc-in1-00000504-001). Includes the Apologia.
Source: Internet Archive — https://archive.org/details/ita-bnc-in1-00000504-001
Rights: Public Domain. Author died 1499; work published 1489, well before 1930. The standard English translation (Kaske & Clark, 1989) is in copyright; no public-domain English translation exists.
Language: Latin
Staged for the Tower of Babel digital library (antarctic-labs.com).
The foundational Renaissance text of astrological/talismanic magic: De vita sana, De vita longa, De vita coelitus comparanda, plus Apologia. OCR of 15th-century print; expect period spelling and OCR noise.
